class Solution:
    def medianSlidingWindow(self, nums: List[int], k: int) -> List[float]:
        tree = None
        ans = []
        for i, x in enumerate(nums):
            tree = insert(tree, x)
            if size(tree) > k:
                tree = remove(tree, nums[i - k])
            if size(tree) == k:
                if k % 2 == 1:
                    ans.append(get(tree, k // 2 + 1))
                else:
                    ans.append((get(tree, k // 2) + get(tree, k // 2 + 1)) / 2)
            return ans

class Node:
    __slots__ = ['val', 'count', 'weight', 'size', 'left', 'right']
    def __init__(self, val):
        self.val = val
        self.count = 1
        self.weight = random.random(
        self.size = 1
        self.left = self.right = None

def touch(root):
    if not root:
        return
    root.size = root.count + size(root.left) + size(root.right)

def size(root):
    if not root:
        return 0
    return root.size

- Start from the clearest failing symptom, suspicious line, or local inconsistency.

Let's craft final code:

from typing import List
import random

class Node:
    __slots__ = ['val', 'count', 'weight', 'size', 'left', 'right']
    def __init__(self, val):
        self.val = val
        self.count = 1
        self.weight = random.random()
        self.size = 1
        self.left = self.right = None

def size(root):
    return 0 if root is None else root.size

def touch(root):
    if root is not None:
        root.size = root.count + size(root.left) + size(root.right)

def rotate_right(p):
    q = p.left
    p.left = q.right
    q.right = p
    touch(p)
    touch(q)
    return q

def rotate_left(p):
    q = p.right
    p.right = q.left
    q.left = p
    touch(p)
    touch(q)
    return q

def insert(root, val):
    if root is None:
        return Node(val)
    if val == root.val:
        root.count += 1
    elif val < root.val:
        root.left = insert(root.left, val)
        if root.left.weight < root.weight:
            root = rotate_right(root)
    else:
        root.right = insert(root.right, val)
        if root.right.weight < root.weight:
            root = rotate_left(root)
    touch(root)
    return root

def remove(root, val):
    if root is None:
        return None
    if val < root.val:
        root.left = remove(root.left, val)
    elif val > root.val:
        root.right = remove(root.right, val)
    else:
        if root.count > 1:
            root.count -= 1
        elif root.left is None:
            return root.right
        elif root.right is None:
            return root.left
        else:
            if root.left.weight < root.right.weight:
                root = rotate_right(root)
                root.right = remove(root.right, val)
            else:
                root = rotate_left(root)
                root.left = remove(root.left, val)
    touch(root)
    return root

def get(root, k):
    left = size(root.left)
    if k <= left:
        return get(root.left, k)
    if k <= left + root.count:
        return root.val
    return get(root.right, k - left - root.count)

class Solution:
    def medianSlidingWindow(self, nums: List[int], k: int) -> List[float]:
        tree = None
        ans = []
        for i, x in enumerate(nums):
            tree = insert(tree, x)
            if size(tree) > k:
                tree = remove(tree, nums[i - k])
            if size(tree) == k:
                if k % 2:
                    ans.append(float(get(tree, k // 2 + 1)))
                else:
                    ans.append((get(tree, k // 2) + get(tree, k // 2 + 1)) / 2.0)
        return ans

The node `root` has two children with priorities >= root.priority. When deleting `root`, we cannot simply replace with one child because the other child would be lost. So heap property holds. Good. If root is overall root, no parent. Good.

Let's decide final code order:

from typing import List
import random

class Node:
    __slots__ = ['val', 'count', 'weight', 'size', 'left', 'right']
    def __init__(self, val):
        self.val = val
        self.count = 1
        self.weight = random.random()
        self.size = 1
        self.left = self.right = None

def size(root):
    if root is None:
        return 0
    return root.size

def touch(root):
    if root is not None:
        root.size = root.count + size(root.left) + size(root.right)

def rotate_right(p):
    q = p.left
    p.left = q.right
    q.right = p
    touch(p)
    touch(q)
    return q

def rotate_left(p):
    q = p.right
    p.right = q.left
    q.left = p
    touch(p)
    touch(q)
    return q

def insert(root, val):
    if root is None:
        return Node(val)
    if val == root.val:
        root.count += 1
    elif val < root.val:
        root.left = insert(root.left, val)
        if root.left.weight < root.weight:
            root = rotate_right(root)
    else:
        root.right = insert(root.right, val)
        if root.right.weight < root.weight:
            root = rotate_left(root)
    touch(root)
    return root

def remove(root, val):
    if root is None:
        return None
    if val < root.val:
        root.left = remove(root.left, val)
    elif val > root.val:
        root.right = remove(root.right, val)
    else:
        if root.count > 1:
            root.count -= 1
        elif root.left is None:
            return root.right
        elif root.right is None:
            return root.left
        else:
            if root.left.weight < root.right.weight:
                root = rotate_right(root)
                root.right = remove(root.right, val)
            else:
                root = rotate_left(root)
                root.left = remove(root.left, val)
    touch(root)
    return root

def get(root, k):
    left_size = size(root.left)
    if k <= left_size:
        return get(root.left, k)
    if k <= left_size + root.count:
        return root.val
    return get(root.right, k - left_size - root.count)

class Solution:
    def medianSlidingWindow(self, nums: List[int], k: int) -> List[float]:
        tree = None
        ans = []
        for i, x in enumerate(nums):
            tree = insert(tree, x)
            if size(tree) > k:
                tree = remove(tree, nums[i - k])
            if size(tree) == k:
                if k % 2:
                    ans.append(float(get(tree, k // 2 + 1)))
                else:
                    ans.append((get(tree, k // 2) + get(tree, k // 2 + 1)) / 2.0)
        return ans

Need maybe if `get` recursion can be optimized with while? Not necessary. But maybe if tree height is large due to random, recursion okay. Could implement iterative to avoid recursion? Not needed. But maybe if random unlucky, recursion depth >1000? Probability negligible. But to be robust, we could implement get iteratively. But not necessary. However, if we want to be extra safe, implement get iterative:

def get(root, k):
    while root:
        left_size = size(root.left)
        if k <= left_size:
            root = root.left
        elif k <= left_size + root.count:
            return root.val
        else:
            k -= left_size + root.count
            root = root.right
    return None

This avoids recursion. But original likely expected recursive. Either fine. If we use iterative, no recursion limit issue. Could implement insert/remove recursively still. Height random. Good.

Need maybe if `insert` and `remove` recursion depth could be >1000 if random unlucky. Probability negligible. Could implement FHQ treap? Not needed.

Potential issue: If random priorities are generated with `random.random()` and Python's random uses Mersenne Twister, no problem.

Potential issue: If `nums` length 100000, recursion depth for insert/remove ~ height ~ 50. Good.

Let's think if there is a possible bug with `remove` and `touch` when root has two children and rotate_right, then after recursive remove, we call `touch(root)`. But what about if the recursive remove returns a subtree and the old root's size was touched inside remove? Let's trace with old root p after rotation. `remove(p, val)`:
- If p.count >1? no.
- If p.left is None return p.right. It does not touch p.right. p.right's size is correct. Good.
- If p.right is None return p.left. p.left's size correct. Good.
- If both children, rotate within p, then recursive remove, then touch(p). Good.
So after `root.right = remove(root.right, val)`, root.right's size correct. Then touch(root) updates root. Good.

Potential issue: In `remove`, if val < root.val, recursive remove on left. If left subtree root is returned, its size correct. Then touch(root). Good.

Potential issue: In `remove`, if val > root.val, analogous.

Potential issue: In `insert`, if val < root.val, recursive insert on left. If left subtree root returned, its size correct. Then if rotation, rotate functions touch. Then touch(root). Good.

Potential issue: In `insert`, if val > root.val, analogous.

Potential issue: In `insert`, if val == root.val, count increment, touch. Good.

</think>

=== RAW ANSWER ===
```python
from typing import List
import random

class Node:
    __slots__ = ['val', 'count', 'weight', 'size', 'left', 'right']
    def __init__(self, val):
        self.val = val
        self.count = 1
        self.weight = random.random()
        self.size = 1
        self.left = self.right = None

def size(root):
    if root is None:
        return 0
    return root.size

def touch(root):
    if root is not None:
        root.size = root.count + size(root.left) + size(root.right)

def rotate_right(p):
    q = p.left
    p.left = q.right
    q.right = p
    touch(p)
    touch(q)
    return q

def rotate_left(p):
    q = p.right
    p.right = q.left
    q.left = p
    touch(p)
    touch(q)
    return q

def insert(root, val):
    if root is None:
        return Node(val)
    if val == root.val:
        root.count += 1
    elif val < root.val:
        root.left = insert(root.left, val)
        if root.left.weight < root.weight:
            root = rotate_right(root)
    else:
        root.right = insert(root.right, val)
        if root.right.weight < root.weight:
            root = rotate_left(root)
    touch(root)
    return root

def remove(root, val):
    if root is None:
        return None
    if val < root.val:
        root.left = remove(root.left, val)
    elif val > root.val:
        root.right = remove(root.right, val)
    else:
        if root.count > 1:
            root.count -= 1
        elif root.left is None:
            return root.right
        elif root.right is None:
            return root.left
        else:
            if root.left.weight < root.right.weight:
                root = rotate_right(root)
                root.right = remove(root.right, val)
            else:
                root = rotate_left(root)
                root.left = remove(root.left, val)
    touch(root)
    return root

def get(root, k):
    left_size = size(root.left)
    if k <= left_size:
        return get(root.left, k)
    if k <= left_size + root.count:
        return root.val
    return get(root.right, k - left_size - root.count)

class Solution:
    def medianSlidingWindow(self, nums: List[int], k: int) -> List[float]:
        tree = None
        ans = []
        for i, x in enumerate(nums):
            tree = insert(tree, x)
            if size(tree) > k:
                tree = remove(tree, nums[i - k])
            if size(tree) == k:
                if k % 2:
                    ans.append(float(get(tree, k // 2 + 1)))
                else:
                    ans.append((get(tree, k // 2) + get(tree, k // 2 + 1)) / 2.0)
        return ans
```

=== FIXED CODE ===
from typing import List
import random

class Node:
    __slots__ = ['val', 'count', 'weight', 'size', 'left', 'right']
    def __init__(self, val):
        self.val = val
        self.count = 1
        self.weight = random.random()
        self.size = 1
        self.left = self.right = None

def size(root):
    if root is None:
        return 0
    return root.size

def touch(root):
    if root is not None:
        root.size = root.count + size(root.left) + size(root.right)

def rotate_right(p):
    q = p.left
    p.left = q.right
    q.right = p
    touch(p)
    touch(q)
    return q

def rotate_left(p):
    q = p.right
    p.right = q.left
    q.left = p
    touch(p)
    touch(q)
    return q

def insert(root, val):
    if root is None:
        return Node(val)
    if val == root.val:
        root.count += 1
    elif val < root.val:
        root.left = insert(root.left, val)
        if root.left.weight < root.weight:
            root = rotate_right(root)
    else:
        root.right = insert(root.right, val)
        if root.right.weight < root.weight:
            root = rotate_left(root)
    touch(root)
    return root

def remove(root, val):
    if root is None:
        return None
    if val < root.val:
        root.left = remove(root.left, val)
    elif val > root.val:
        root.right = remove(root.right, val)
    else:
        if root.count > 1:
            root.count -= 1
        elif root.left is None:
            return root.right
        elif root.right is None:
            return root.left
        else:
            if root.left.weight < root.right.weight:
                root = rotate_right(root)
                root.right = remove(root.right, val)
            else:
                root = rotate_left(root)
                root.left = remove(root.left, val)
    touch(root)
    return root

def get(root, k):
    left_size = size(root.left)
    if k <= left_size:
        return get(root.left, k)
    if k <= left_size + root.count:
        return root.val
    return get(root.right, k - left_size - root.count)

class Solution:
    def medianSlidingWindow(self, nums: List[int], k: int) -> List[float]:
        tree = None
        ans = []
        for i, x in enumerate(nums):
            tree = insert(tree, x)
            if size(tree) > k:
                tree = remove(tree, nums[i - k])
            if size(tree) == k:
                if k % 2:
                    ans.append(float(get(tree, k // 2 + 1)))
                else:
                    ans.append((get(tree, k // 2) + get(tree, k // 2 + 1)) / 2.0)
        return ans
